Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The matter operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 294. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 902 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_294(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 294. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^902(S, F) is trivial. QED.
